How to read this report
  • A run's score is the weighted fraction of its graders that passed; a "perfect" run passed every grader.
  • A case's score is the mean of its runs; a case passes when its score is at or above the 100% threshold (the tick on each case bar).
  • The suite score is the mean of the per-case scores.
  • Judge votes are independent samples of the LLM judge; the majority decides pass or fail.
  • No baseline arm was run (ablation off) — this report shows absolute scores only and cannot say whether the plugin caused them. Re-run with --ablation with-without to measure the plugin’s effect.

# UI Audit — Reminders (2026-11-02)

## Stack
- Framework: Vanilla JS + FastAPI serving one Jinja2 template (`app/templates/index.html`)
- Build tool: none
- CSS: plain CSS in `app/static/styles.css` (13 lines) plus inline `style=` in the template
- State: none (the list is re-fetched and re-rendered as HTML strings; the server keeps an in-memory list)
- Design tokens: `app/static/styles.css` (`:root` with 4 variables)
- Run: `uvicorn app.main:app --reload` (run from the project root, because the paths are relative to it) → http://127.0.0.1:8000 (uvicorn's default; no README or Procfile confirms it)
- Checks: none found

## Pages / Routes (top 10)
SPA mode: one page, three client-side tabs.
- `/` — `app/templates/index.html` (the only HTML page)
- Tab "Today" — `#today` in `index.html`, filled by `app/static/app.js` (reminders not yet done)
- Tab "All reminders" — `#all` in `index.html`, filled by `app/static/app.js`
- Tab "Settings" — `#settings` in `index.html` (notification time field and Save button)
- `/api/reminders` (GET) — `app/main.py` (data for the tabs, not a page)
- `/api/reminders/{id}/done` (POST) — `app/main.py` (the "Done" button)

## Style approach
Styling is split between a tiny stylesheet and inline `style=` attributes on 12 elements in the template. Colors are hard-coded hex values (about 17 distinct). The four `--color-*` / `--space-2` tokens in `:root` are defined but never used with `var(...)`.

## Pain points

### 🔴 High
- Not responsive: `<body>` is fixed at `width: 800px`, `.view` at `760px`, the settings input at `300px`, and there is no `<meta name="viewport">`. On a phone the page is cut off or zoomed out.
- Tokens exist but are unused: `--color-primary` and `--color-success` hold the same values as the hard-coded `#2b6cb0` and `#38a169`, but nothing references them. Wiring the tokens in is the highest-leverage fix for the new look.
- Accessibility basics missing: no `alt` on the logo `<img>` (and `/static/logo.png` does not exist, so it shows a broken image), no `lang` on `<html>`, no `<header>`, `<nav>` or `<main>`, no `aria-` attributes at all, and the tabs are plain buttons with no selected state shown to the user or to screen readers.
- Contrast: white on the green Save button (`#38a169`, about 3.3:1) and the grey `#777` helper text and `#a0aec0` finished items are below the 4.5:1 readable minimum.
- Settings does nothing real: "Save" only shows a browser `alert()`. Nothing is sent to or stored by the backend. The redesign must keep this behaviour visible or the Developer must add an endpoint.

### 🟡 Medium
- No loading or error state: the reminders `fetch` (page load) and the Done `fetch` (`markDone`) show no spinner, disabled button or error message; the tabs stay blank until data arrives or if the request fails.
- No empty state: `.empty` is styled in the CSS but never used, so an empty Today or All tab is a blank area.
- "Today" lists every not-done reminder, regardless of its due date. Confirm with the Stakeholder whether that is intended before the design shows "today" differently.
- Reminder titles are inserted with `innerHTML` without escaping (`app.js`). A title containing HTML would be rendered as markup. This is a Developer matter, not a design one.
- The reminder rows show a "Done" button even on rows already done in the "All reminders" tab.

### 🟢 Low
- Hard-coded English text throughout; no i18n markers. Fine for a one-language app, so only a note.
- Tab and button styles are set partly in CSS and partly inline (for example three different grey text colors on the tab buttons), so they look inconsistent.
- No `<title>` beyond "Reminders", no favicon, no page description.

## Recommendation
Confirm Scenario C: the stack is tiny and healthy (all files well under 500 lines), so keep FastAPI + Jinja + vanilla JS and restyle it. Note for the brief: the design must cover the three tabs, the Done action, and the Save action in Settings (currently not persisted).

# UI Audit — Reminders (2026-11-02)

## Stack
- Framework: FastAPI serving one Jinja2 template (`index.html`) with vanilla JS
- Build tool: none
- CSS: plain CSS in `app/static/styles.css`, plus inline `style=` in the template
- State: none (DOM re-rendered from `/api/reminders`; data held in an in-memory list in `app/main.py`)
- Design tokens: `app/static/styles.css` (`:root` with 4 variables)
- Run: `uvicorn app.main:app` from the project root (inferred from the entry point; no README or script states it) → http://127.0.0.1:8000 (uvicorn default)
- Checks: none found

## Pages / Routes (top 10)
Single-page app: one page, three client-side tabs.
- `/` — `app/templates/index.html` (page shell, served by `app/main.py`)
- Tab "Today" — `#today` view, unfinished reminders (`app/static/app.js`)
- Tab "All reminders" — `#all` view, every reminder (`app/static/app.js`)
- Tab "Settings" — `#settings` view, notification time (`app/templates/index.html`)
- `/api/reminders` (GET), `/api/reminders/{id}/done` (POST) — `app/main.py` (data only, no UI)

## Style approach
Most styling is inline in the template (11 `style=` attributes), with a small stylesheet next to it. The stylesheet defines colour and spacing tokens, but nothing uses them; every colour is hard-coded. About 16 distinct colours are in use, several of them near-duplicate blues and greys.

## Pain points

### 🔴 High
- Not responsive: `body` is fixed at `width: 800px` and `.view` at `760px`. There is no `<meta viewport>` and no media queries, so the page breaks on phones.
- Tokens exist but are unused: `--color-primary`, `--color-success`, `--color-text`, `--space-2` are defined, yet none is referenced (`var(` count is 0) while colours are repeated inline (`#2b6cb0`, `#38a169`) and in CSS (`#3182ce` for buttons, a different blue).
- No accessibility basics: no `lang` on `<html>`, no `<header>`/`<nav>`/`<main>`, tabs are plain buttons with no `role="tab"`/`aria-selected`, the notification input has no label, and `<img src="/static/logo.png">` has no `alt`.
- Broken logo: `app/static/logo.png` does not exist, so the header shows a broken image.
- Reminder titles are inserted into the page with `innerHTML` unescaped (`app/static/app.js`), so a title containing HTML would be rendered as markup. It is a code issue the Developer should know about, and the redesign should not carry it over.

### 🟡 Medium
- No loading or error state: the reminders list is empty until the fetch returns, and a failed fetch or "Done" click shows nothing. The "Done" button stays active while the request runs. All three spot-checked calls (`loadReminders`, `markDone`, none for Save) lack this.
- Settings "Save" does nothing real: it only shows a browser `alert()`. No backend route exists for it, so a design cannot assume a working setting (matters for the integration plan).
- No empty state: `.empty` is defined in CSS but never used, so an empty Today list is a blank area.
- No tests, lint or typecheck, so `design-port` has nothing automatic to run before showing a Screen.

### 🟢 Low
- Tab buttons give no sign of which tab is active, and tab text colours (`#333`, `#444`, `#555`) differ without reason.
- Text is hard-coded English with no i18n markers (fine if the app stays single-language).
- Page `<title>` is the generic "Reminders" and there is no favicon.

## Recommendation
Confirm scenario C: the app is tiny (about 100 lines in total), the stack is simple, and a new look can be ported into the existing template and stylesheet. One thing for the integration plan: the design may show data or actions the backend lacks (settings save, due dates, add/edit/delete reminders).

# UI Audit — Reminders (2026-11-02)

## Stack
- Framework: Vanilla JS + FastAPI serving one Jinja2 template
- Build tool: none
- CSS: plain CSS in `app/static/styles.css` plus inline `style=` in the template
- State: none (DOM re-rendered from `GET /api/reminders`; server keeps an in-memory list)
- Design tokens: `app/static/styles.css` (`:root` with 4 variables)
- Run: `uvicorn app.main:app --reload` (run from the project root) → http://127.0.0.1:8000 (inferred from the entry point; no README or script states it)
- Checks: none found

## Pages / Routes (top 10)
Single-page app; tabs are the pages the user sees.
- `/` — `app/templates/index.html` (route in `app/main.py`)
- Tab "Today" — `#today` in `app/templates/index.html`, filled by `app/static/app.js`
- Tab "All reminders" — `#all`, same files
- Tab "Settings" — `#settings`, same files (Save only shows an `alert`; nothing is stored)
- `/api/reminders` (GET) — `app/main.py`
- `/api/reminders/{id}/done` (POST) — `app/main.py`

## Style approach
Styling is split between a small stylesheet and inline `style=` attributes in the template. Design tokens are defined (`--color-primary`, `--color-success`, `--color-text`, `--space-2`) but no rule uses them; colors are written as hex values instead.

## Pain points

### 🔴 High
- Not responsive: `<body>` is fixed at `width: 800px` and `.view` at `760px`, and the Settings input at `300px`. There is no media query. The page will overflow on a phone.
- Missing viewport meta tag and `lang` on `<html>`, so phones render it as a zoomed-out desktop page.
- Tokens exist but are unused: `:root` has 4 variables with 0 `var(--…)` uses, while 13 inline `style=` attributes and 12 distinct hex colors are hard-coded (`#2b6cb0` and `#38a169` duplicate the tokens). Wiring these up is the highest-leverage fix.
- Accessibility: `<img src="/static/logo.png">` has no `alt` (and no logo file exists in `app/static/`). The tabs are plain `<button>`s with no `role="tab"` or `aria-selected`. There is no `<header>`, `<nav>` or `<main>`, no `<label>` on the notification-time input, and no visible active-tab state.
- No loading, error or empty state: `fetch("/api/reminders")` and the POST in `markDone` show nothing while waiting and ignore failures. `.empty` is styled but never used, so an empty list shows blank space.

### 🟡 Medium
- Reminder titles are inserted with `innerHTML` unescaped, so any title containing HTML is rendered as markup (XSS risk once reminders can be created).
- The "Today" tab lists every not-done reminder, not only those due today, so the name is misleading.
- Settings "Save" only calls `alert()` and persists nothing. It needs a real confirmation state, and the backend has no endpoint for it.
- Hard-coded English text, with no i18n markers (`data-i18n`, `t()`), if the product needs other languages.
- Low contrast: `.reminder.done` text `#a0aec0` and the settings label `#777` on `#fafafa` are below the 4.5:1 contrast ratio.

### 🟢 Low
- Inconsistent button styling: the tab, "Done" and "Save" buttons each use different colors and padding.
- Every "Done" button looks the same, including on rows that are already done (the button is still shown and clickable).
- `<title>` is generic, and there is no favicon.

## Recommendation
Confirm scenario C: the app is tiny (about 100 lines total, no oversized files) and the stack is simple, so keep FastAPI + Jinja and restyle it in place.

<!-- Generated by /ui-workflow-skills:design-brief on 2026-11-02. Scenario: C. Design tool: Google AI Studio. -->

=== COPY EVERYTHING BELOW INTO Google AI Studio ===

You are designing the **look** of an existing web application. A developer will rebuild your design by hand in the project's existing stack (FastAPI + Jinja2 templates, plain CSS, vanilla JavaScript). Your code is read as a visual reference for its layout, styles and copy. It is never run or shipped, so only the look matters.

## What to design

Design only the look: **layout, spacing, colours, fonts and component states** (hover, focus, disabled, active, open, empty, loading, error).

## How to build it

- Prefer **plain semantic HTML** (`<header>`, `<nav>`, `<main>`, `<section>`, `<form>`, `<button>`) and **one CSS file** that defines every colour, spacing, font, radius and shadow as a **CSS variable**.
- If your tool always builds with a framework, that is fine. Keep components shallow and keep every style value readable in the source.
- Avoid markup that only makes sense for one component library.
- Build **one page or route per Screen** listed below. When several Screens share one page today (tabs, steps), still give each Screen its own page or route.
- **No routing logic, no state management, no data fetching, no real backend, no auth.** Use static sample content.
- Describe every interaction in a plain-English comment next to the element, for example:
  ```html
  <!-- interaction: clicking Done marks this reminder as done -->
  ```

## Screens to redesign (only the look changes)
The app is a small reminders tool. Today all three Screens live as tabs on one page; give each its own page or route, and show the same three-tab navigation on every one of them.
- `Today` — the reminders that are not done yet, each with a Done button. This is the Screen people open first.
- `All reminders` — every reminder, done and not done. Done ones look finished (muted, struck through). Every row has a Done button.
- `Settings` — one field, the time of day the user wants to be notified, and a Save button.

## Real copy and field names (use these exactly)
Shared header (on every Screen):
- App name: `Reminders` (bold text only; there is no logo image)
- Greeting: `Hello {owner}`, for example `Hello Lan`
- Tabs: `Today`, `All reminders`, `Settings`

`Today` and `All reminders`:
- Each reminder row reads `{title} — {due}`, for example `Pay rent — 2026-11-01`
- Data fields per reminder: `id`, `title`, `due` (a date written as YYYY-MM-DD), `done` (true or false)
- Row button: `Done`
- Sample data: `Pay rent` due `2026-11-01`, not done; `Call dentist` due `2026-11-03`, done
- `Today` must show only the reminders where `done` is false. In the sample data that is `Pay rent` alone.
- `All reminders` shows both sample reminders.
- Empty state: the project has no empty text yet. Show a short, friendly line such as `Nothing to do today` on `Today` and `No reminders yet` on `All reminders`.

`Settings`:
- Label: `Notification time`
- Field: a time input (id `notify-time`)
- Button: `Save`

## What needs to look better
- The page is a fixed 800px wide and scrolls sideways on phones. It must fit any phone width and also look good on a desktop.
- The tabs give no sign of which one is active. Make the active tab obvious.
- There is no empty state when there are no reminders. Design one for `Today` and `All reminders`.
- Colours are hard-coded all over the place. Use the tokens below instead.

## Target users
About 20 office staff using this internally. They are not technical. They open the app mostly on their phone, and also on a computer.

## Most important user actions
1. See what needs doing today (the `Today` Screen).
2. Mark a single reminder as done with the `Done` button.
3. Set the time of day they receive notifications (the `Settings` Screen).

## Brand

- **Primary color**: #2b6cb0 — used for primary buttons, links, the active tab, focus rings
- **Secondary / accent color**: none given; do not add one. Keep the interface to the primary colour plus neutrals.
- **Neutral palette**: a calm cool-grey scale from near-white to #1a202c (Tailwind `slate`/`gray` 50 → 900 is fine)
- **Success / warning / danger**: success #38a169 (used for the done state and for confirming a Save); warning and danger not specified, use muted standard tones and only if a state needs them
- **Body font family**: Inter, system-ui, sans-serif
- **Heading font family**: same as body
- **Logo**: text-only logo: `Reminders` in Inter, bold
- **Tone**: minimal
- **Reference sites/apps** (for feel — do NOT clone): Todoist, Things 3

## Visual personality
Calm, quiet and minimal, like Things 3 for its spacing and Todoist for its clear task rows. Lots of white space. Few colours, soft borders, no gradients, no emoji, no decoration that does not help the user act.

## Constraints

### Responsive
- **Mobile-first** design
- Breakpoints: `640px` (tablet), `1024px` (desktop), `1280px` (wide)
- Tap targets minimum **44 × 44 px**
- No horizontal scroll on any viewport ≥ 320px
- Device priority: mobile first and most important, desktop must also work well

### Accessibility
- **WCAG 2.1 AA** minimum
- Every `<img>` has an `alt` attribute
- Form fields have **visible labels** (not placeholder-only)
- Contrast ratio ≥ **4.5:1** for body text, ≥ 3:1 for large text
- **Focus states are visible** for keyboard users (custom or browser default — never `outline: none`)
- Interactive elements are real `<button>` / `<a>` (not styled `<div>`s)

### Performance
- No animations longer than 300ms
- No autoplay video
- Lazy-load images below the fold (`loading="lazy"`)
- No blocking 3rd-party scripts above the fold

### Browser support
- Latest Chrome, Safari, Firefox, Edge (last 2 versions)
- iOS Safari 15+
- Android Chrome 100+
- **No IE support required**

### State coverage (required for every async interaction)
1. **Loading** — visible feedback during fetch / submit
2. **Success** — default rendered state
3. **Empty** — explicit empty-state UI (not a blank page)
4. **Error** — readable error message + a retry path where applicable

### Hard constraints
- none

## Existing design tokens (REUSE — do not invent new colors)
```css
:root {
  --color-primary: #2b6cb0;
  --color-success: #38a169;
  --color-text: #1a202c;
  --space-2: 8px;
}
```

## Deliverable
- One page or route per Screen above, with the real copy and field names.
- One CSS file with the design tokens as CSS variables at the top.
- Every component state above shown at least once.
- A plain-English interaction comment on every interactive element.
- Mobile-first responsive layout with breakpoints at `640px` and `1024px`.

## One shared style
This may be the only message I can send, so build everything now.
- Choose one visual style yourself and use it on every Screen, from the same CSS file and variables.
- Build every Screen above in this first build. Do not stop after the first Screen, and do not ask me to choose anything first.
- Build one version of each Screen: no alternative versions, variations or style switchers.

## Out of scope (do not produce)
- Routing, state management, data fetching, backend or server code, auth, database, API keys
- Screens, features or fields not listed above
- Extra dependencies or build configuration beyond what your tool needs by default

=== END COPY ===

## Required behaviour

### Today — app/templates/index.html#today
- Shows: reminders where `done` is false, from `GET /api/reminders` (filtered in `app/static/app.js`). Each row shows `title` and `due`. A `Hello {{ owner }}` greeting and a logo image (`/static/logo.png`, no alt text) sit in the header above the tabs.
- Does: `Done` button on each row calls `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`, then the list reloads and the row leaves Today. No empty state today.

### All reminders — app/templates/index.html#all
- Shows: every reminder from `GET /api/reminders` with `title` and `due`. Done ones are muted and struck through (`.reminder.done`).
- Does: `Done` button on every row, including rows already done, calls `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`, then the list reloads. No empty state today.

### Settings — app/templates/index.html#settings
- Shows: a `Notification time` label, a text input `#notify-time` and a `Save` button.
- Does: nothing lasting. `Save` only shows a browser alert `Saved <value>`; no endpoint stores the time.

### Stakeholder must-haves
- Each reminder can still be marked done individually with its own Done button.
- The Today tab shows only reminders that are not done.
- Top actions: see today's tasks, mark one as done, set the notification time.

✅ Mình đã lưu prompt vào `.ui-workflow/design-prompt.md`. Bạn mở file, copy toàn bộ phần giữa hai dòng `=== COPY ===` rồi dán vào Google AI Studio (Build).

**Trước khi dán:** vào Settings của AI Studio. Nếu có tuỳ chọn HTML thuần thì chọn nó. Nếu không thì chọn framework nhẹ nhất (React, đừng chọn Next.js hay Angular). Mình chưa kiểm chứng được tuỳ chọn này, nên nếu Settings không có lựa chọn nào như vậy, bạn cứ dán, framework nào cũng dùng được.

**Sau khi hài lòng với kết quả:** tải về dạng ZIP hoặc đồng bộ lên GitHub. Rồi quay lại chạy `/ui-workflow-skills:ingest-design-export`.

**Trong prompt có:**
- Cả ba màn hình (Hôm nay, Tất cả, Cài đặt) với đúng chữ hiện có trong app: `Today`, `All reminders`, `Settings`, `Notification time`, `Save`, `Done`.
- Màu #2b6cb0 và #38a169, font Inter, chữ "Reminders" đậm thay cho logo, cảm giác tối giản như Todoist và Things 3.
- Ưu tiên điện thoại nhưng máy tính vẫn dùng tốt.
- Hai điều bắt buộc: mỗi nhắc việc vẫn có nút Done riêng, và tab Today chỉ hiện việc chưa xong.

**Ba điểm bạn nên biết:**
- App hiện chưa có chữ cho trạng thái trống. Mình gợi ý `Nothing to do today` và `No reminders yet`. Bạn đổi lại nếu muốn câu khác.
- Nút `Save` ở Cài đặt hiện chỉ hiện một hộp thoại, chưa lưu giờ thông báo ở đâu cả. Thiết kế mới sẽ trông đúng, nhưng chức năng này cần developer làm riêng. Mình đã ghi chuyện này trong phần "Required behaviour" của file.
- Tab "Tất cả" hiện có nút Done cả ở việc đã xong. Mình ghi lại đúng như vậy, không tự thay đổi.

Trạng thái workflow đã cập nhật: công cụ là Google AI Studio, bước tiếp theo là `ingest`.

## Required behaviour

### Today — app/templates/index.html#today
- Shows: reminders where `done` is false, from `GET /api/reminders` (filtered in `app/static/app.js`). Each row shows `title` and `due`. A `Hello {{ owner }}` greeting and a logo image (`/static/logo.png`, no alt text) sit in the header above the tabs.
- Does: `Done` button on each row calls `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`, then the list reloads and the row leaves Today. No empty state today.

### All reminders — app/templates/index.html#all
- Shows: every reminder from `GET /api/reminders` with `title` and `due`. Done ones are muted and struck through (`.reminder.done`).
- Does: `Done` button on every row, including rows already done, calls `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`, then the list reloads. No empty state today.

### Settings — app/templates/index.html#settings
- Shows: a `Notification time` label, a text input `#notify-time` and a `Save` button.
- Does: nothing lasting. `Save` only shows a browser alert `Saved <value>`; no endpoint stores the time.

### Stakeholder must-haves
- Each reminder can still be marked done individually with its own Done button.
- The Today tab shows only reminders that are not done.
- Top actions: see today's tasks, mark one as done, set the notification time.

=== COPY EVERYTHING BELOW INTO Google AI Studio ===

You are designing the **look** of an existing web application called "Reminders". A developer will rebuild your design by hand in the project's existing stack (FastAPI + Jinja2 templates, plain CSS, vanilla JavaScript). Your code is read as a visual reference for its layout, styles and copy. It is never run or shipped, so only the look matters.

## What to design

Design only the look: **layout, spacing, colours, fonts and component states** (hover, focus, disabled, active, open, empty, loading, error).

## How to build it

- Prefer **plain semantic HTML** (`<header>`, `<nav>`, `<main>`, `<section>`, `<form>`, `<button>`) and **one CSS file** that defines every colour, spacing, font, radius and shadow as a **CSS variable**.
- If your tool always builds with a framework, that is fine. Keep components shallow and keep every style value readable in the source.
- Avoid markup that only makes sense for one component library.
- Build **one page or route per Screen** listed below. When several Screens share one page today (tabs, steps), still give each Screen its own page or route. All three Screens below are tabs of one page today; give each its own page or route, each showing the same header and the same tab bar with the correct tab marked active.
- **No routing logic, no state management, no data fetching, no real backend, no auth.** Use static sample content.
- Describe every interaction in a plain-English comment next to the element, for example:
  ```html
  <!-- interaction: clicking Done marks this reminder as done -->
  ```

## Screens to redesign (only the look changes)
- `Today` — the list of reminders that are not done yet, each with its title, its due date and a Done button. The user can mark a reminder as done. Reminders that are done never appear here.
- `All reminders` — the list of every reminder, done and not done, each with its title, its due date and a Done button. Done reminders look visibly finished (muted, struck through). The user can mark a reminder as done.
- `Settings` — one setting: the time of day the user wants to be notified. The user types a time and saves it.

## Real copy and field names (use these exactly)

**Page header (on every Screen)**
- Text logo: "Reminders" in bold. There is no logo image; do not draw an icon or a logo mark.
- Greeting: "Hello {{ owner }}". Use the sample name "Lan" in the static sample.

**Tab bar (on every Screen)**
- "Today"
- "All reminders"
- "Settings"

**Today and All reminders**
- Each row shows: `title` and `due`, followed by a button labelled "Done".
- Row text pattern today: "<title> — <due>", for example "Pay rent — 2026-11-01".
- Data fields per reminder: `id` (number, not displayed), `title` (text), `due` (date shown as YYYY-MM-DD), `done` (true or false; true means the row looks finished).
- Sample rows to use:
  - "Pay rent", due "2026-11-01", not done
  - "Call dentist", due "2026-11-03", done (appears only on All reminders)
- Empty state: the project has no empty-state copy yet. Design one for each list with a short, calm sample line, and mark it as sample text.

**Settings**
- Label: "Notification time"
- One text input for the time (field name `notify-time`)
- Button: "Save"
- Give the input a visible label (not placeholder-only).

## What needs to look better
- The page is a fixed 800px wide and scrolls sideways on phones. It must fit any phone width with no horizontal scroll.
- Colours are hard-coded all over the markup instead of coming from variables. Use the CSS variables below for every colour.
- Tabs give no sign of which one is active. Show the active tab clearly.
- There is no empty state when there are no reminders. Design one.

## Target users
Internal tool for about 20 office staff at one company. They are not technical. Most open it on a phone to see what they need to do today, and some use it on a laptop. They need to understand it at a glance, with nothing to learn.

## Most important user actions
1. See what has to be done today (the Today screen is the first thing they see).
2. Mark a reminder as done with one clear tap.
3. Set the time they get notified (Settings).

## Brand

- **Primary color**: #2b6cb0 (blue) — used for the active tab, primary actions, links and focus rings
- **Secondary / accent color**: none; do not add one
- **Neutral palette**: a calm cool-grey scale from near-white to near-black (for example 6 to 7 greys); text uses #1a202c
- **Success / warning / danger**: success #38a169 (green) — used for the "done" state, for example the check mark on a completed reminder and the confirmation after saving; warning and danger: use a soft amber and a soft red, only where truly needed
- **Body font family**: Inter, with system-ui as fallback
- **Heading font family**: same as body (Inter)
- **Logo**: text-only logo: "Reminders" in Inter, bold
- **Tone**: minimal
- **Reference sites/apps** (for feel — do NOT clone): Todoist, Things 3

## Visual personality (free-form, 2–3 lines)
Feel like a calm, quiet to-do app. Minimal, with a lot of white space and generous spacing between rows, so a short list feels relaxed, not empty. No gradients, no heavy shadows, no emoji, and only subtle, short transitions.

## Constraints

### Responsive
- **Mobile-first** design
- Breakpoints: `640px` (tablet), `1024px` (desktop), `1280px` (wide)
- Tap targets minimum **44 × 44 px**
- No horizontal scroll on any viewport ≥ 320px
- Device priority: mobile is the most important; desktop must also work well (on wide screens keep the content in a comfortable centred column, do not stretch rows edge to edge)

### Accessibility
- **WCAG 2.1 AA** minimum
- Every `<img>` has an `alt` attribute
- Form fields have **visible labels** (not placeholder-only)
- Contrast ratio ≥ **4.5:1** for body text, ≥ 3:1 for large text
- **Focus states are visible** for keyboard users (custom or browser default — never `outline: none`)
- Interactive elements are real `<button>` / `<a>` (not styled `<div>`s)

### Performance
- No animations longer than 300ms
- No autoplay video
- Lazy-load images below the fold (`loading="lazy"`)
- No blocking 3rd-party scripts above the fold

### Browser support
- Latest Chrome, Safari, Firefox, Edge (last 2 versions)
- iOS Safari 15+
- Android Chrome 100+
- **No IE support required** unless explicitly noted below

### State coverage (required for every async interaction)
1. **Loading** — visible feedback during fetch / submit
2. **Success** — default rendered state
3. **Empty** — explicit empty-state UI (not a blank page)
4. **Error** — readable error message + a retry path where applicable

### Hard constraints
- Marking a reminder as done must stay possible for each reminder individually: every row on Today and on All reminders keeps its own "Done" button.
- The Today screen shows only reminders that are not done. Design it so a finished reminder is not in that list.
- None other.

## Existing design tokens (REUSE — do not invent new colors)
```css
:root {
  --color-primary: #2b6cb0;
  --color-success: #38a169;
  --color-text: #1a202c;
  --space-2: 8px;
}
```
Keep these four variables with these names and values, and add your own variables (neutral greys, more spacing steps, radius, font) next to them in the same `:root` block.

## Deliverable
- One page or route per Screen above, with the real copy and field names.
- One CSS file with the design tokens as CSS variables at the top.
- Every component state above shown at least once.
- A plain-English interaction comment on every interactive element.
- Mobile-first responsive layout with breakpoints at `640px` and `1024px`.

## One shared style
This may be the only message I can send, so build everything now.
- Choose one visual style yourself and use it on every Screen, from the same CSS file and variables.
- Build every Screen above in this first build. Do not stop after the first Screen, and do not ask me to choose anything first.
- Build one version of each Screen: no alternative versions, variations or style switchers.

## Out of scope (do not produce)
- Routing, state management, data fetching, backend or server code, auth, database, API keys
- Screens, features or fields not listed above
- Extra dependencies or build configuration beyond what your tool needs by default

=== END COPY ===

## Required behaviour

### Today — app/templates/index.html#today
- Shows: the reminders from `GET /api/reminders` (fields `id`, `title`, `due`, `done`) that have `done` false, one row each as "<title> — <due>"; page header "Hello {{ owner }}" (`owner` is a template variable passed by `index()` in app/main.py). Done reminders are filtered out in `loadReminders()` in app/static/app.js. There is no empty state today.
- Does: "Done" button on each row calls `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`, then the lists reload and the row leaves Today. Tab buttons switch views client-side.

### All reminders — app/templates/index.html#all
- Shows: every reminder from `GET /api/reminders` (`id`, `title`, `due`, `done`), as "<title> — <due>"; rows with `done` true are muted and struck through (`.reminder.done`). No empty state today.
- Does: "Done" button on every row (also on rows that are already done) calls `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`, then the lists reload.

### Settings — app/templates/index.html#settings
- Shows: label "Notification time", a text input `#notify-time`, and a "Save" button. The input starts empty; nothing is loaded from the backend.
- Does: none that lasts. "Save" only shows a browser alert "Saved <value>"; nothing is sent to or stored by the backend (no endpoint exists for it).

### Stakeholder must-haves
- Each reminder can still be marked as done individually (the "Done" button per row).
- The Today tab shows only reminders that are not done.
- Setting a notification time must be possible (the stakeholder's top-3 action; today the Save control only shows an alert, so it needs a real backend later).

✅ Mình đã lưu prompt vào `.ui-workflow/design-prompt.md`. Bạn mở file, chép phần giữa hai dòng `=== COPY ===` rồi dán vào Google AI Studio (Build).

**Trước khi dán:** mở Settings của AI Studio. Nếu có tùy chọn HTML thuần thì chọn nó. Nếu không thì chọn framework nhẹ nhất, tức React thay vì Next.js hay Angular. Mình chưa kiểm chứng được tùy chọn này. Nếu không thấy lựa chọn nào, bạn cứ dán, vì framework nào cũng dùng được.

**Trong prompt:**
- **Phạm vi:** prompt chỉ xin phần giao diện cho cả ba tab (Today, All reminders, Settings), với văn bản và tên trường lấy đúng từ `index.html`.
- **Thương hiệu:** màu chính #2b6cb0, xanh lá #38a169 cho trạng thái xong, font Inter. Logo chỉ là chữ "Reminders" đậm. Cảm giác tối giản, bình tĩnh, nhiều khoảng trắng, tham khảo Todoist và Things 3.
- **Thiết bị:** ưu tiên điện thoại, máy tính vẫn dùng tốt.
- **Hai điều bắt buộc:** mỗi nhắc việc giữ nút "Done" riêng, và tab Hôm nay chỉ hiện việc chưa xong.
- **Màu có sẵn:** bốn biến màu trong `styles.css` được giữ nguyên tên và giá trị.
- **Các lỗi cũ cần sửa:** trang cố định 800px, màu viết cứng, tab không báo đang chọn, chưa có trạng thái trống.

**Hai điểm cần lưu ý:**
- **Nút Save:** nút Save ở Settings hiện chỉ hiện hộp thoại báo "Saved…", không lưu gì và backend chưa có endpoint cho việc này. Đặt giờ thông báo là một trong ba việc quan trọng của bạn, nên cần làm phần backend sau. Mình đã ghi chú trong phần "Required behaviour".
- **Câu cho trạng thái trống:** dự án chưa có câu nào cho trạng thái trống, nên prompt để AI Studio tự viết câu mẫu và đánh dấu là chữ mẫu.

Mình đã cập nhật `state.md`: công cụ là Google AI Studio, bước hiện tại là `ingest`. Khi bạn ưng kết quả, hãy tải về dạng ZIP hoặc đồng bộ lên GitHub, rồi chạy `/ui-workflow-skills:ingest-design-export`.

- Each row shows: `title` and `due`, followed by a button labelled "Done".
- Row text pattern today: "<title> — <due>", for example "Pay rent — 2026-11-01".
- Data fields per reminder: `id` (number, not displayed), `title` (text), `due` (date shown as YYYY-MM-DD), `done` (true or false; true means the row looks finished).
- Sample rows to use:
  - "Pay rent", due "2026-11-01", not done
  - "Call dentist", due "2026-11-03", done (appears only on All reminders)
- Empty state: the project has no empty-state copy yet. Design one for each list with a short, calm sample line, and mark it as sample text.

**Settings**
- Label: "Notification time"
- One text input for the time (field name `notify-time`)
- Button: "Save"
- Give the input a visible label (not placeholder-only).

## What needs to look better
- The page is a fixed 800px wide and scrolls sideways on phones. It must fit any phone width with no horizontal scroll.
- Colours are hard-coded all over the markup instead of coming from variables. Use the CSS variables below for every colour.
- Tabs give no sign of which one is active. Show the active tab clearly.
- There is no empty state when there are no reminders. Design one.

## Target users
Internal tool for about 20 office staff at one company. They are not technical. Most open it on a phone to see what they need to do today, and some use it on a laptop. They need to understand it at a glance, with nothing to learn.

## Most important user actions
1. See what has to be done today (the Today screen is the first thing they see).
2. Mark a reminder as done with one clear tap.
3. Set the time they get notified (Settings).

## Brand

- **Primary color**: #2b6cb0 (blue) — used for the active tab, primary actions, links and focus rings
- **Secondary / accent color**: none; do not add one
- **Neutral palette**: a calm cool-grey scale from near-white to near-black (for example 6 to 7 greys); text uses #1a202c
- **Success / warning / danger**: success #38a169 (green) — used for the "done" state, for example the check mark on a completed reminder and the confirmation after saving; warning and danger: use a soft amber and a soft red, only where truly needed
- **Body font family**: Inter, with system-ui as fallback
- **Heading font family**: same as body (Inter)
- **Logo**: text-only logo: "Reminders" in Inter, bold
- **Tone**: minimal
- **Reference sites/apps** (for feel — do NOT clone): Todoist, Things 3

## Visual personality (free-form, 2–3 lines)
Feel like a calm, quiet to-do app. Minimal, with a lot of white space and generous spacing between rows, so a short list feels relaxed, not empty. No gradients, no heavy shadows, no emoji, and only subtle, short transitions.

## Constraints

### Responsive
- **Mobile-first** design
- Breakpoints: `640px` (tablet), `1024px` (desktop), `1280px` (wide)
- Tap targets minimum **44 × 44 px**
- No horizontal scroll on any viewport ≥ 320px
- Device priority: mobile is the most important; desktop must also work well (on wide screens keep the content in a comfortable centred column, do not stretch rows edge to edge)

### Accessibility
- **WCAG 2.1 AA** minimum
- Every `<img>` has an `alt` attribute
- Form fields have **visible labels** (not placeholder-only)
- Contrast ratio ≥ **4.5:1** for body text, ≥ 3:1 for large text
- **Focus states are visible** for keyboard users (custom or browser default — never `outline: none`)
- Interactive elements are real `<button>` / `<a>` (not styled `<div>`s)

### Performance
- No animations longer than 300ms
- No autoplay video
- Lazy-load images below the fold (`loading="lazy"`)
- No blocking 3rd-party scripts above the fold

### Browser support
- Latest Chrome, Safari, Firefox, Edge (last 2 versions)
- iOS Safari 15+
- Android Chrome 100+
- **No IE support required** unless explicitly noted below

### State coverage (required for every async interaction)
1. **Loading** — visible feedback during fetch / submit
2. **Success** — default rendered state
3. **Empty** — explicit empty-state UI (not a blank page)
4. **Error** — readable error message + a retry path where applicable

### Hard constraints
- Marking a reminder as done must stay possible for each reminder individually: every row on Today and on All reminders keeps its own "Done" button.
- The Today screen shows only reminders that are not done. Design it so a finished reminder is not in that list.
- None other.

## Existing design tokens (REUSE — do not invent new colors)
```css
:root {
  --color-primary: #2b6cb0;
  --color-success: #38a169;
  --color-text: #1a202c;
  --space-2: 8px;
}
```
Keep these four variables with these names and values, and add your own variables (neutral greys, more spacing steps, radius, font) next to them in the same `:root` block.

## Deliverable
- One page or route per Screen above, with the real copy and field names.
- One CSS file with the design tokens as CSS variables at the top.
- Every component state above shown at least once.
- A plain-English interaction comment on every interactive element.
- Mobile-first responsive layout with breakpoints at `640px` and `1024px`.

## One shared style
This may be the only message I can send, so build everything now.
- Choose one visual style yourself and use it on every Screen, from the same CSS file and variables.
- Build every Screen above in this first build. Do not stop after the first Screen, and do not ask me to choose anything first.
- Build one version of each Screen: no alternative versions, variations or style switchers.

## Out of scope (do not produce)
- Routing, state management, data fetching, backend or server code, auth, database, API keys
- Screens, features or fields not listed above
- Extra dependencies or build configuration beyond what your tool needs by default

=== END COPY ===

## Required behaviour

### Today — app/templates/index.html#today
- Shows: the reminders from `GET /api/reminders` (fields `id`, `title`, `due`, `done`) that have `done` false, one row each as "<title> — <due>"; page header "Hello {{ owner }}" (`owner` is a template variable passed by `index()` in app/main.py). Done reminders are filtered out in `loadReminders()` in app/static/app.js. There is no empty state today.
- Does: "Done" button on each row calls `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`, then the lists reload and the row leaves Today. Tab buttons switch views client-side.

### All reminders — app/templates/index.html#all
- Shows: every reminder from `GET /api/reminders` (`id`, `title`, `due`, `done`), as "<title> — <due>"; rows with `done` true are muted and struck through (`.reminder.done`). No empty state today.
- Does: "Done" button on every row (also on rows that are already done) calls `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`, then the lists reload.

### Settings — app/templates/index.html#settings
- Shows: label "Notification time", a text input `#notify-time`, and a "Save" button. The input starts empty; nothing is loaded from the backend.
- Does: none that lasts. "Save" only shows a browser alert "Saved <value>"; nothing is sent to or stored by the backend (no endpoint exists for it).

### Stakeholder must-haves
- Each reminder can still be marked as done individually (the "Done" button per row).
- The Today tab shows only reminders that are not done.
- Setting a notification time must be possible (the stakeholder's top-3 action; today the Save control only shows an alert, so it needs a real backend later).

=== COPY EVERYTHING BELOW INTO Google AI Studio ===

You are designing the **look** of an existing web application. A developer will rebuild your design by hand in the project's existing stack (FastAPI + Jinja2 templates, plain CSS, vanilla JavaScript). Your code is read as a visual reference for its layout, styles and copy. It is never run or shipped, so only the look matters.

## What to design

Design only the look: **layout, spacing, colours, fonts and component states** (hover, focus, disabled, active, open, empty, loading, error).

## How to build it

- Prefer **plain semantic HTML** (`<header>`, `<nav>`, `<main>`, `<section>`, `<form>`, `<button>`) and **one CSS file** that defines every colour, spacing, font, radius and shadow as a **CSS variable**.
- If your tool always builds with a framework, that is fine. Keep components shallow and keep every style value readable in the source.
- Avoid markup that only makes sense for one component library.
- Build **one page or route per Screen** listed below. When several Screens share one page today (tabs, steps), still give each Screen its own page or route.
- **No routing logic, no state management, no data fetching, no real backend, no auth.** Use static sample content.
- Describe every interaction in a plain-English comment next to the element, for example:
  ```html
  <!-- interaction: clicking Done marks this reminder as done -->
  ```

## Screens to redesign (only the look changes)

The app is a small reminders tool. Today all three Screens are tabs of one page; give each its own page or route, and repeat the same header and tab bar on each one.

- `Today` — the reminders that are not done yet. Each row shows the reminder's title and due date and has a Done button. Done reminders never appear here.
- `All reminders` — every reminder, done and not done. Each row shows the title and due date and a Done button. A done reminder looks visibly finished (muted and struck through).
- `Settings` — one setting: the time of day the user wants to be notified. A time field and a Save button.

## Real copy and field names (use these exactly)

Shared header (on every Screen):
- Text logo: `Reminders` (bold, no image logo)
- Greeting: `Hello Lan` (the name `Lan` comes from the template variable `owner`)
- Tab bar labels: `Today`, `All reminders`, `Settings`

`Today`:
- Row content: `<title> — <due>`, for example `Pay rent — 2026-11-01`
- Button: `Done`
- Data fields per reminder: `id`, `title`, `due` (date as YYYY-MM-DD), `done` (true or false)
- Sample rows: `Pay rent — 2026-11-01` (not done), `Call dentist — 2026-11-03` (not done)
- Empty state (no reminders left): the project has no empty-state text yet. Design one with a short, friendly placeholder line.

`All reminders`:
- Same row content and `Done` button as `Today`
- Sample rows: `Pay rent — 2026-11-01` (not done), `Call dentist — 2026-11-03` (done: muted and struck through)
- Empty state: same as `Today`

`Settings`:
- Label: `Notification time`
- Field: a time input (id `notify-time`)
- Button: `Save`

Mình đã viết xong prompt và lưu vào `.ui-workflow/design-prompt.md`. Prompt viết bằng tiếng Anh, đúng như công cụ thiết kế cần.

**Cách dùng:**
1. Mở file, copy phần nằm giữa hai dòng `=== COPY EVERYTHING BELOW INTO Google AI Studio ===` và `=== END COPY ===`.
2. Dán vào Google AI Studio (Build).
3. Trước khi dán, vào Settings. Nếu có tuỳ chọn HTML thuần thì chọn nó. Nếu không thì chọn framework nhẹ nhất, tức React thay vì Next.js hay Angular. Mình chưa thử bước này, nên nếu không thấy tuỳ chọn nào thì cứ dán, framework nào cũng dùng được.
4. Khi ưng ý, tải về dạng ZIP hoặc đồng bộ lên GitHub, rồi chạy `/ui-workflow-skills:ingest-design-export`.

**Prompt đã đưa vào những gì:**
- **Ba màn hình:** Hôm nay (Today), Tất cả (All reminders) và Cài đặt (Settings). Mỗi màn là một trang riêng, dùng chung header và thanh tab.
- **Chữ trên giao diện:** lấy từ template hiện tại như `Hello Lan`, `Done`, `Notification time`, `Save`. Mình giữ nguyên tiếng Anh vì app đang dùng tiếng Anh.
- **Thương hiệu:** #2b6cb0 là màu chính, #38a169 cho trạng thái hoàn thành, font Inter, logo là chữ `Reminders` đậm.
- **Cảm giác:** tối giản, bình tĩnh, nhiều khoảng trắng, tham khảo Todoist và Things 3 (chỉ lấy cảm giác, không sao chép).
- **Thiết bị:** ưu tiên điện thoại, và trên máy tính hiển thị một cột căn giữa cho dễ đọc.
- **Lỗi hiện tại cần sửa:**
  - Trang rộng cố định 800px nên bị cuộn ngang trên điện thoại.
  - Màu đang gắn cứng trong HTML thay vì dùng biến.
  - Tab không cho biết đang ở tab nào.
  - Chưa có màn hình trống.
- **Bắt buộc:** vẫn đánh dấu xong từng nhắc việc, và tab Hôm nay chỉ hiện việc chưa xong. Hai yêu cầu này nằm trong phần "Required behaviour", phần này nằm ngoài vùng copy và để các bước sau đọc.

**Hai điều cần để ý:**
- **Nút Save chưa lưu gì.** Ở code hiện tại, nút Save trong Cài đặt chỉ hiện hộp thoại "Saved …", chứ không lưu giờ thông báo ở đâu cả. Mình ghi rõ điều này trong "Required behaviour". Vì "đặt giờ nhận thông báo" là một trong ba việc quan trọng nhất, bạn nên nhờ lập trình viên làm phần lưu thật.
- **Màn hình trống chưa có chữ.** App chưa có câu nào cho trường hợp không còn nhắc việc. Mình để công cụ thiết kế tự đặt một câu ngắn, thân thiện. Nếu bạn muốn câu cụ thể, hãy sửa trong file trước khi dán.

Mình cũng đã cập nhật trạng thái quy trình: công cụ thiết kế là Google AI Studio, và bước tiếp theo là nhập bản xuất (ingest).

<!-- Generated by /ui-workflow-skills:design-brief on 2026-11-02. Scenario: C. Design tool: Google AI Studio. -->

=== COPY EVERYTHING BELOW INTO Google AI Studio ===

You are designing the **look** of an existing web application. A developer will rebuild your design by hand in the project's existing stack (FastAPI + Jinja2 templates, plain CSS, vanilla JavaScript). Your code is read as a visual reference for its layout, styles and copy. It is never run or shipped, so only the look matters.

## What to design

Design only the look: **layout, spacing, colours, fonts and component states** (hover, focus, disabled, active, open, empty, loading, error).

## How to build it

- Prefer **plain semantic HTML** (`<header>`, `<nav>`, `<main>`, `<section>`, `<form>`, `<button>`) and **one CSS file** that defines every colour, spacing, font, radius and shadow as a **CSS variable**.
- If your tool always builds with a framework, that is fine. Keep components shallow and keep every style value readable in the source.
- Avoid markup that only makes sense for one component library.
- Build **one page or route per Screen** listed below. When several Screens share one page today (tabs, steps), still give each Screen its own page or route.
- **No routing logic, no state management, no data fetching, no real backend, no auth.** Use static sample content.
- Describe every interaction in a plain-English comment next to the element, for example:
  ```html
  <!-- interaction: clicking Done marks this reminder as done -->
  ```

## Screens to redesign (only the look changes)

The app is a small reminders tool. Today all three Screens are tabs of one page; give each its own page or route, and repeat the same header and tab bar on each one.

- `Today` — the reminders that are not done yet. Each row shows the reminder's title and due date and has a Done button. Done reminders never appear here.
- `All reminders` — every reminder, done and not done. Each row shows the title and due date and a Done button. A done reminder looks visibly finished (muted and struck through).
- `Settings` — one setting: the time of day the user wants to be notified. A time field and a Save button.

## Real copy and field names (use these exactly)

Shared header (on every Screen):
- Text logo: `Reminders` (bold, no image logo)
- Greeting: `Hello Lan` (the name `Lan` comes from the template variable `owner`)
- Tab bar labels: `Today`, `All reminders`, `Settings`

`Today`:
- Row content: `<title> — <due>`, for example `Pay rent — 2026-11-01`
- Button: `Done`
- Data fields per reminder: `id`, `title`, `due` (date as YYYY-MM-DD), `done` (true or false)
- Sample rows: `Pay rent — 2026-11-01` (not done), `Call dentist — 2026-11-03` (not done)
- Empty state (no reminders left): the project has no empty-state text yet. Design one with a short, friendly placeholder line.

`All reminders`:
- Same row content and `Done` button as `Today`
- Sample rows: `Pay rent — 2026-11-01` (not done), `Call dentist — 2026-11-03` (done: muted and struck through)
- Empty state: same as `Today`

`Settings`:
- Label: `Notification time`
- Field: a time input (id `notify-time`)
- Button: `Save`

## What needs to look better

- The page is a fixed 800px wide and scrolls sideways on phones. It must fit any width from 320px up.
- Colours are hard-coded inline instead of coming from variables. Use CSS variables only.
- Nothing shows which tab is active. Make the active tab clear.
- There is no empty state when there are no reminders. Design one.

## Target users

About 20 office staff using this internal tool every day. They are not technical. They mostly use it on their phones, and also on a computer.

## Most important user actions

1. See what needs doing today (the `Today` Screen, listing only reminders that are not done).
2. Mark a reminder as done with the `Done` button, one reminder at a time.
3. Set the time they get notified (the `Settings` Screen).

## Brand

- **Primary color**: #2b6cb0 — used for primary buttons, links, the active tab and focus rings
- **Secondary / accent color**: none — do not add one
- **Neutral palette**: a calm cool-grey scale of 5–7 greys, with #1a202c as the text colour
- **Success / warning / danger**: success #38a169 (used for the done state and successful actions); warning and danger are not needed, and if an error state needs a colour use a muted red
- **Body font family**: Inter
- **Heading font family**: same as body (Inter)
- **Logo**: text-only logo: `Reminders` in bold Inter
- **Tone**: minimal
- **Reference sites/apps** (for feel — do NOT clone): Todoist, Things 3

## Visual personality (free-form, 2–3 lines)
Minimal and calm, like a quiet personal to-do app, not a busy dashboard. Lots of white space, few colours, one clear action per row. No gradients, no emoji, no heavy shadows.

## Constraints

### Responsive
- **Mobile-first** design
- Breakpoints: `640px` (tablet), `1024px` (desktop), `1280px` (wide)
- Tap targets minimum **44 × 44 px**
- No horizontal scroll on any viewport ≥ 320px
- Device priority: mobile first, and desktop must work just as well (on desktop use a comfortable centred column, not a stretched full-width list)

### Accessibility
- **WCAG 2.1 AA** minimum
- Every `<img>` has an `alt` attribute
- Form fields have **visible labels** (not placeholder-only)
- Contrast ratio ≥ **4.5:1** for body text, ≥ 3:1 for large text
- **Focus states are visible** for keyboard users (custom or browser default — never `outline: none`)
- Interactive elements are real `<button>` / `<a>` (not styled `<div>`s)

### Performance
- No animations longer than 300ms
- No autoplay video
- Lazy-load images below the fold (`loading="lazy"`)
- No blocking 3rd-party scripts above the fold

### Browser support
- Latest Chrome, Safari, Firefox, Edge (last 2 versions)
- iOS Safari 15+
- Android Chrome 100+
- **No IE support required** unless explicitly noted below

### State coverage (required for every async interaction)
1. **Loading** — visible feedback during fetch / submit
2. **Success** — default rendered state
3. **Empty** — explicit empty-state UI (not a blank page)
4. **Error** — readable error message + a retry path where applicable

### Hard constraints
- none

## Existing design tokens (REUSE — do not invent new colors)
```css
:root {
  --color-primary: #2b6cb0;
  --color-success: #38a169;
  --color-text: #1a202c;
  --space-2: 8px;
}
```
Keep these variable names and values. Add your own variables (more greys, spacing steps, radius, font) next to them in the same `:root` block.

## Deliverable
- One page or route per Screen above, with the real copy and field names.
- One CSS file with the design tokens as CSS variables at the top.
- Every component state above shown at least once.
- A plain-English interaction comment on every interactive element.
- Mobile-first responsive layout with breakpoints at `640px` and `1024px`.

## One shared style
This may be the only message I can send, so build everything now.
- Choose one visual style yourself and use it on every Screen, from the same CSS file and variables.
- Build every Screen above in this first build. Do not stop after the first Screen, and do not ask me to choose anything first.
- Build one version of each Screen: no alternative versions, variations or style switchers.

## Out of scope (do not produce)
- Routing, state management, data fetching, backend or server code, auth, database, API keys
- Screens, features or fields not listed above
- Extra dependencies or build configuration beyond what your tool needs by default

=== END COPY ===

## Required behaviour

### Today — app/templates/index.html#today
- Shows: reminders from `GET /api/reminders` (fields `id`, `title`, `due`, `done`), only those with `done` false, as `<title> — <due>`. Also the shared header with `Hello {{ owner }}` (`owner` is a template variable served by `GET /`).
- Does: `Done` button on each row calls `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`, then the list reloads and the row leaves this tab.

### All reminders — app/templates/index.html#all
- Shows: every reminder from `GET /api/reminders`, as `<title> — <due>`; done ones get the `done` style (muted, struck through).
- Does: `Done` button on each row calls `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`, then the list reloads. Done rows keep their `Done` button today.

### Settings — app/templates/index.html#settings
- Shows: label `Notification time` and a text input `notify-time`.
- Does: `Save` only shows a browser alert `Saved <value>`. It stores nothing and calls no endpoint today, so the notification time is not saved anywhere.

### Tab switching (shared by all three Screens)
- Does: clicking a tab button shows that view and hides the others, client-side in `app/static/app.js`. No sign of the active tab today.

### Stakeholder must-haves
- Each reminder can still be marked done individually, from its own row.
- The Today tab shows only reminders that are not done.

Here's the code Google AI Studio gave me. Skip the screenshots.

index.html:

<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <body>
    <div id="root"></div>
    <script type="module" src="/index.tsx"></script>
  </body>
</html>

index.tsx:

import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import App from "./App";

createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(<App />);

App.tsx:

import { useState } from "react";

type View = "today" | "all" | "settings";

export default function App() {
  const [view, setView] = useState<View>("today");
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50">
      <nav className="flex gap-2 bg-indigo-600 p-4 text-white">
        <button onClick={() => setView("today")}>Today</button>
        <button onClick={() => setView("all")}>All reminders</button>
        <button onClick={() => setView("settings")}>Settings</button>
      </nav>
      {view === "today" && (
        <ul className="p-6">
          <li className="rounded-xl bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">Call the dentist</li>
        </ul>
      )}
      {view === "all" && (
        <table className="m-6 w-full rounded-xl bg-white">
          <tbody>
            <tr><td>Call the dentist</td><td>Today</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Renew passport</td><td>Next week</td></tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      )}
      {view === "settings" && (
        <form className="m-6 space-y-4 rounded-xl bg-white p-6">
          <label>Notification time <input type="time" /></label>
          <button className="rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 px-4 py-2 text-white">Save</button>
        </form>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

Màn **Today** đã được chuyển sang thiết kế mới, nhưng tôi chưa mở được nó trong app nên chưa có gì được commit. Sandbox không cho app mở cổng cục bộ 8000, lỗi `PermissionError: Operation not permitted`. Vì vậy tôi chưa xem được màn hình, kể cả ở 390px và 1440px, và bạn cũng chưa có đường link để xem. Màn này đang chờ được mở lên cho bạn duyệt. Tôi đã ghi lý do này vào `Blocked` trong `.ui-workflow/state.md`.

**Kiểm tra dự án:** bài test duy nhất (`tests/test_css.py`, kiểm tra stylesheet không còn rule thừa) đã chạy và đạt.

**Việc cần Developer làm:** chạy `python3 app/main.py` ở máy ngoài sandbox, hoặc bật `sandbox.network.allowLocalBinding: true` trong settings. Sau đó mở http://127.0.0.1:8000, tab Today. Đây là việc của Developer, bạn không cần tự chạy gì.

**Những gì đã làm trên nhánh `ui-workflow/2026-11-02`:**
- **Các file đã sửa:**
  - `app/templates/index.html`: giao diện Today, danh sách và trạng thái trống "Nothing due today."
  - `app/static/styles.css`: thêm 3 màu mới (paper, ink, stamp), các kiểu cho Today, và thay chiều rộng cố định 800px/760px bằng tối đa 48rem.
  - `.ui-workflow/state.md`
- **Dữ liệu:** vẫn lấy từ `GET /api/reminders`. Nút Done vẫn gọi `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`.
- **Tương phản:** chữ ink trên nền paper đạt 13.5:1, chữ trắng trên nút stamp đạt 9.4:1. Thẻ trắng trên nền paper chỉ đạt 1.11:1, giống thiết kế gốc, nên viền thẻ chỉ dựa vào bóng đổ.
- **Ảnh tư liệu "founders-1931.jpg":** đây chỉ là một ảnh gradient nâu xám, không phải ảnh thật. Tên file và alt text lại mô tả nó như ảnh lưu trữ có thật. Tôi chưa copy nó vào dự án và để một ô nền trắng trơn ở vị trí đó.
- **Những thứ thiết kế ghi như sự thật nhưng tôi bỏ đi:** "A family habit since 1931", "Trusted by 12,480 households", "Source: Hanoi City Archive, ref. HCA-1931-0472" và huy hiệu "Verified by the Heritage Board". Dự án không có dữ liệu nào cho chúng.
- **Các tab khác bị ảnh hưởng:** nền paper, chữ ink và độ rộng tối đa mới được áp cho cả trang. Vì vậy All reminders và Settings cũng đổi nền, màu chữ và độ rộng. Nội dung bên trong hai tab đó chưa đổi.
- **Cho Developer:** export không ghim phiên bản Tailwind, nên tôi giả định v3. Font giữ nguyên font mặc định của app. Không có screenshot trong export. Thay đổi trong `app/main.py` (thêm "Buy milk") và `notes.txt` là của bạn, tôi không đụng tới.

Bạn có muốn dùng ảnh gradient đó không? Tôi sẽ chỉ copy nó vào dự án nếu bạn đồng ý. Sau khi Developer mở được app, bạn duyệt màn Today hay cần sửa gì? Duyệt xong tôi mới commit.

I've ported the Today screen, but you can't approve it yet. The app wouldn't start in my environment, so I haven't seen the screen running. Nothing is committed.

**Checks:** The one project check (stylesheet has no unused rules) passes. Nothing else could be verified. I couldn't open the page or look at it at phone and laptop widths, so the layout and the "Done" button are untested.

**Developer needed:** My sandbox refuses local port binding (`PermissionError: Operation not permitted`). Someone needs to allow `sandbox.network.allowLocalBinding: true`, or run `python3 app/main.py` and open http://127.0.0.1:8000 (Today tab). I've recorded this in `Blocked` in `.ui-workflow/state.md`.

**What changed** (on branch `ui-workflow/2026-11-02`):
- `app/templates/index.html`: Today now has a heading, an empty-state line ("Nothing due today.") and a list of reminders with `title`, `due` and a Done button. The Done button calls the existing `markDone`.
- `app/static/styles.css`: new tokens for the paper, ink and accent colours, plus the Today styles.

**Shared shell** (the other tabs inherit these):
- The page background is now the cream "paper" colour.
- The fixed 800px width is now a 768px maximum, so it shrinks on phones.
- The page uses a system font.
- All reminders and Settings keep their old look, but now sit on the cream background and the narrower width.

**Left out of the design:**
- **Invented claims:** "A family habit since 1931", "Trusted by 12,480 households", the Hanoi City Archive source line and reference code, and the "Verified by the Heritage Board" badge. They're filler, and no backend field supplies them.
- **Photo:** The design includes a photo labelled as an original 1931 archival photograph. It's probably a placeholder, so I left a plain empty band where it goes and didn't copy the file in.
- **Not reproduced:** the photo.

**Developer notes:**
- **Tailwind version:** The design loads Tailwind from a CDN with no version pinned, so I assumed v3 defaults.
- **Contrast:** Ink on paper is 13.5:1 and white on the accent colour is 9.4:1. White rows on the paper background are only 1.1:1, so the rows are separated by their shadow, as in the design.
- **Your uncommitted changes:** `app/main.py` (the extra "Buy milk" reminder) and `notes.txt` are untouched and won't go into the commit.

Two questions for you:
1. Do you want the archival photo? It's not in your project's data, so I'd only copy it in if you say yes.
2. Once someone has run the app and you've looked at Today, do you approve it, or what should change?

Tôi đã port xong màn hình **Today**, nhưng chưa cho bạn xem được. App không khởi động được trong môi trường này, nên màn hình chưa được duyệt và chưa commit.

**Việc cần Developer xử lý**
- Sandbox chặn mở cổng cục bộ (`PermissionError: Operation not permitted` khi chạy `python3 app/main.py`). Nên xem màn hình này chưa được: tôi không xem được ở 390px hay 1440px, và không có ảnh chụp màn hình nào để đối chiếu.
- Cách xử lý: bật `sandbox.network.allowLocalBinding: true` trong settings, hoặc tự chạy `python3 app/main.py` rồi mở http://127.0.0.1:8000. Tôi không tự đổi cài đặt sandbox.
- Bài test duy nhất trong `tests/` (`test_css.py`, kiểm tra class CSS nào không dùng) đã chạy và **pass**. Nó không kiểm tra giao diện hiển thị.

**Đã làm**
- Tôi tạo nhánh làm việc `ui-workflow/2026-11-02` và ghi vào `state.md`, kèm lý do bị chặn ở mục `Blocked`.
- `app/static/styles.css`:
  - Thêm 4 token mới: `--color-paper`, `--color-ink`, `--color-stamp`, `--color-surface`.
  - Thêm các class `.today-*` cho tiêu đề, danh sách, thẻ, nút Done và dòng "Nothing due today."
  - Thêm style cho `body` (rộng tối đa 48rem, nền giấy).
  - Thêm viền focus rõ cho nút Done và các nút tab.
  - Độ tương phản đã tính bằng script: chữ ink trên nền giấy 13.5:1, chữ trắng trên nút stamp 9.4:1.
- `app/templates/index.html`: view Today giờ gồm tiêu đề, một khung trơn, danh sách thẻ trắng đổ bóng và nút Done màu stamp. Dữ liệu vẫn lấy từ `GET /api/reminders`, và nút Done vẫn gọi `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`. Dòng "Nothing due today." hiện khi không còn việc nào.

**Ảnh tư liệu: tôi chưa đưa vào, cần bạn quyết**
- File `founders-1931.jpg` thực chất chỉ là một dải màu xám nâu chuyển sắc, không phải ảnh lưu trữ. Thiết kế gắn nhãn nó là "Original archival photograph of the founders' office, Hanoi, 1931".
- Vì vậy chỗ đó tôi để một khung trơn, chưa copy ảnh vào dự án. Bạn có muốn dùng ảnh này, hay có ảnh thật của riêng bạn?

**Nội dung thiết kế tôi bỏ vì là số liệu minh hoạ, app không có dữ liệu thật cho chúng**
- "A family habit since 1931. Trusted by 12,480 households."
- "Source: Hanoi City Archive, ref. HCA-1931-0472."
- Huy hiệu "Verified by the Heritage Board".

**Những chỗ khác với thiết kế hoặc ảnh hưởng tab khác**
- Nền giấy, màu chữ và chiều rộng 48rem nằm ở khung chung của trang, nên hai tab "All reminders" và "Settings" cũng đổi theo. Tôi bỏ `width: 800px` cố định ở `body` và `width: 760px` ở `.view`, nên trên điện thoại sẽ không còn bị tràn ngang.
- Nút Done có đệm lớn hơn thiết kế một chút để dễ bấm trên điện thoại.
- Thẻ trắng trên nền giấy chỉ đạt tương phản 1.11:1. Đây là ý đồ của thiết kế (chỉ phân biệt bằng bóng đổ).
- Với Developer: thiết kế dùng Tailwind CDN không ghim phiên bản, nên tôi giả định v3 khi quy đổi giá trị bóng đổ và cỡ chữ.

Bạn chạy app được rồi thì mở http://127.0.0.1:8000 (tab Today) và cho tôi biết: **duyệt màn hình này, hay cần chỉnh gì?** Sau khi bạn duyệt, tôi mới commit.

- Thẻ trắng trên nền giấy chỉ đạt tương phản 1.11:1. Đây là ý đồ của thiết kế (chỉ phân biệt bằng bóng đổ).
- Với Developer: thiết kế dùng Tailwind CDN không ghim phiên bản, nên tôi giả định v3 khi quy đổi giá trị bóng đổ và cỡ chữ.

Bạn chạy app được rồi thì mở http://127.0.0.1:8000 (tab Today) và cho tôi biết: **duyệt màn hình này, hay cần chỉnh gì?** Sau khi bạn duyệt, tôi mới commit.

**What changed** (on branch `ui-workflow/2026-11-02`):
- `app/templates/index.html`: new Today markup, a new Today renderer, and the fixed 800px page width removed.

- A "Today" heading with the italic "What needs you now" line.

- Loading, error and empty states, which are new.

- Snooze is a disabled button labelled "coming soon".
- Share list is dropped.

**Substitutes and things not matched:**
- **Fonts:** the design uses Fraunces and Inter from Google Fonts. I added no external font link, so it falls back to the system sans and Georgia italic.
- **Panel width:** the design's side-panel width came from a file missing from the export. I took 16rem from the screenshot.
- **Muted text:** the design's grey text was 4.3:1 on the background, below the 4.5 minimum. I made it darker (5.9:1).
- **New colours:** I added tokens for the teal accent and the warm background, because the existing green fails contrast with white text.
- **Card borders:** the design's soft card borders are only about 1.2:1 against the background. I kept them as designed. Want them darker?
- **Icons:** the bell, check and clock are inline SVGs in the style of the Lucide icon set (ISC licence). I wrote them from memory of its published shapes, so check they look right.
- **Left out:** the "Share list" button and the export's Today-only snooze logic.

**Shared page changes:**
- The page is now up to 56rem wide and cream-coloured, and the Settings and All reminders tabs inherit that.
- All reminders keeps its old row markup until it's ported.
- I didn't touch `app/main.py`, `notes.txt` or Settings.
- Your brief asked for a Done view, but the plan lists only Today and All reminders, so nothing covers it yet.

**Files changed:** `app/templates/index.html` and `app/static/styles.css`. `.ui-workflow/state.md` now records the branch and the blocker.

**Decisions applied from the plan:**
- Snooze is a disabled button labelled "Snooze (coming soon)".
- Share list is dropped.
- The "At a glance" count comes from the real reminders.

**Page-wide changes:** the page is now up to 56rem wide and uses the new cream background. That replaces the fixed 800px width, so All reminders, Settings and the top bar inherit it. The width of the content area also changed.

**Substitutes and things left out:**
- **Icons:** I couldn't download the design's icon set (network blocked). I used a "✓" and a 🔔 character instead, and left out the clock icon.
- **Fonts:** Inter and Fraunces are named in the stylesheet but not loaded, so the browser falls back to system fonts, as in the design's own screenshot.
- **Subtitle color:** the design pulls it from a file that wasn't exported. I used the same muted text color as the due dates.
- **Muted text:** the design's grey (60%) failed contrast at 4.05:1, so I used 65%, which gives 4.6:1.
- **Card borders:** the borders on reminder cards are very faint (about 1.2:1), as in the design. I kept them because the cards don't depend on the border to be recognised. Say if you want them darker.
- **Mobile layout:** below 768px the "At a glance" panel goes full width under the list. The design doesn't show a narrow layout.
- **Focus outlines:** I added a visible focus outline to buttons, which the design removed.
- **Fetch errors:** I added a "We could not load your reminders." message when loading fails.

- Run `python3 app/main.py` from the project root. I'd also like the screen viewed at 390px and 1440px.

## Today screen

It is built but uncommitted, on the new branch `ui-workflow/2026-11-02`. It will be at http://127.0.0.1:8000, on the Today tab, once the app runs.

**Files changed:** `app/templates/index.html` and `app/static/styles.css`. `.ui-workflow/state.md` now records the branch and the blocker.

**Decisions applied from the plan:**
- Snooze is a disabled button labelled "Snooze (coming soon)".
- Share list is dropped.
- The "At a glance" count comes from the real reminders.

**Page-wide changes:** the page is now up to 56rem wide and uses the new cream background. That replaces the fixed 800px width, so All reminders, Settings and the top bar inherit it. The width of the content area also changed.

**Substitutes and things left out:**
- **Icons:** I couldn't download the design's icon set (network blocked). I used a "✓" and a 🔔 character instead, and left out the clock icon.
- **Fonts:** Inter and Fraunces are named in the stylesheet but not loaded, so the browser falls back to system fonts, as in the design's own screenshot.
- **Subtitle color:** the design pulls it from a file that wasn't exported. I used the same muted text color as the due dates.
- **Muted text:** the design's grey (60%) failed contrast at 4.05:1, so I used 65%, which gives 4.6:1.
- **Card borders:** the borders on reminder cards are very faint (about 1.2:1), as in the design. I kept them because the cards don't depend on the border to be recognised. Say if you want them darker.
- **Mobile layout:** below 768px the "At a glance" panel goes full width under the list. The design doesn't show a narrow layout.
- **Focus outlines:** I added a visible focus outline to buttons, which the design removed.
- **Fetch errors:** I added a "We could not load your reminders." message when loading fails.

**For the Developer:**
- Run `python3 app/main.py` from the project root. I'd also like the screen viewed at 390px and 1440px.
- No Tailwind version assumption was needed (3.4.10 is pinned).
- I read `screenshots/today.png`.
- There is no `Checks` line in `audit.md`, so I ran the only suite, `tests/test_css.py`.
- I left your uncommitted change to `app/main.py` and `notes.txt` alone.

Once the app is running, can you check Today and tell me whether to approve it or what to change? All reminders is next.

No Preview link is ready yet, and nothing was published. Vercel refused the preview deploy because this project has never been deployed. Its message was "first_deployment_production". Vercel assigns a project's first deployment to Production, and this CLI (65.9.0) won't create a preview in that state. I didn't retry with `--prod`, because that would put the new UI on a production address.

**What I did:**
- The frontend is a static Vite build that calls a public API, so Vercel is the right host.
- I linked the project to Vercel as `reminders-web`. `VITE_API_URL` was already set for Preview.
- The production address (`reminders-web.vercel.app`) returns 404, so nothing is live there.
- I recorded the failure in `.ui-workflow/preview.md` and set `Blocked` in `.ui-workflow/state.md`.
- I added `.ui-workflow/preview.md` to a new `.gitignore`.

**A Developer needs to do this once.** It puts an empty placeholder in production, so none of the new UI reaches it:
1. In an empty folder, copy this project's `.vercel/` folder.
2. Add `public/index.html` containing an empty page.
3. Add `vercel.json` containing `{ "framework": null, "buildCommand": null, "outputDirectory": "public" }`.
4. Run `vercel deploy --prod --skip-domain`. `--skip-domain` leaves the production alias unassigned.

After that, run `/ui-workflow-skills:preview-deploy` again. I'll deploy a real preview and check it from outside.

Vercel also protects previews with a login page by default. You'll need to create a Shareable Link: Vercel dashboard → Deployments → this preview → Share → "Anyone with the link". Without it, your phone will land on a Vercel login page.

2. Add a `public/index.html` containing an empty page.
3. Add a `vercel.json` containing `{ "framework": null, "buildCommand": null, "outputDirectory": "public" }`.
4. Run `vercel deploy --prod --skip-domain`. The `--skip-domain` flag leaves the production alias unassigned.

After that, run `/ui-workflow-skills:preview-deploy` again.

Vercel protects previews with a login page by default, so your phone would hit that wall. Whoever owns the Vercel project will also need to create a Shareable Link for the preview. In the dashboard, open Deployments, pick the preview, choose Share, then "Anyone with the link", then Copy Link.

I recorded the failure in `.ui-workflow/preview.md` and in `Blocked` in `.ui-workflow/state.md`. I also added `.ui-workflow/preview.md` to a new `.gitignore`.

- **`.npmrc` của export:** tôi cũng không copy nó, vì có thể chứa token. Project hiện không có dependency nên không cần nó.

I haven't connected the design to the backend, because that needs the API key in the frontend. I stopped there and changed no code.

**Why I stopped.** The backend checks a shared key (`REMINDERS_API_KEY`, sent in the `X-API-Key` header). Anything in frontend config is shipped to the browser. Once the Preview is up, anyone with the link could read the key and call your API as you. This workflow doesn't allow that, even for a Preview. I also haven't read the key's value from your environment, since you didn't hand it to me for this.

**What I did.** I only edited `.ui-workflow/state.md`. It now says the wire step is blocked, with the reason. No frontend folder, branch or commit has been created.

**What needs a Developer.** The calls need to go through a server-side route, so the key stays on the server. Two small additions to the FastAPI app would do it:
- a proxy route for `GET /api/reminders`
- a proxy route for `POST /api/reminders/{id}/done`

Each route would add the `X-API-Key` header itself. The new frontend would then call those routes with no key. That would also make the Preview work, because the key never leaves the server.

I can write those routes, but they change the backend (`app/main.py`, which already has uncommitted edits), and this step isn't allowed to touch the backend. Do you want me to add them? If you do, say so and I'll lift the block. Or a Developer can add them and re-run `/ui-workflow-skills:integration-wire`.

- the adapter from the backend's `{id, title, due, done}` to the design's `{id, name, dueDate, isDone}`

**For a Developer**
- **Secret removed:** The Coach screen read an OpenAI key from `VITE_OPENAI_API_KEY` and called OpenAI straight from the browser. That would have shipped the key to every visitor, so I removed that call. The "Ask the coach" button now returns a sample answer. I did not copy the export's `.env`, which holds that variable, and I did not read its value. If the key is real, rotate it. A real coach needs a server-side route.
- **Build failure:** `npm install` was refused with a 403 when it tried to download `@ui-workflow-eval/design-kit`, which the build script imports. In this sandbox the registry is blocked, so `npm run build` fails on the missing package. I did not stub or remove that import to get a green build. A Developer needs to make the package installable, then re-run `/ui-workflow-skills:integration-wire`.
